multiplying and averaging over the convex combination gives the claim.


K      Lean formalization
A subset of the discrete results is formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib (tag v4.32.0). The
formalization is in the directory lean/, in the library InfLearn. It has 17 modules and about
14,600 lines.
    • There is no sorry, no axiom declaration and no native_decide.
    • lake build reports no errors and no warnings.
    • lake build Audit runs #print axioms on 483 main declarations. Each depends only
      on the standard axioms propext, Classical.choice and Quot.sound, or on a subset of
      them. The output is in lean/audit-output.txt.

                                                 258
Theorems carrying the tag Lean: . . . in the text are formalized in the sense of Table 24. The column
“fidelity” takes one of four values:
   • exact: the paper statement, possibly in a more general form;
   • special case: a strict special case, as described in lean/README.md;
   • weaker: a weaker conclusion than the paper states;
   • variant: a closely related statement, neither stronger nor weaker.

Modelling conventions. The following conventions apply throughout.
   • Language. The object language is propositional, with atoms p0 , p1 , . . . and the connectives
     ⊥, ⊤, ¬, ∧, ∨, →. Results stated for arbitrary Boolean signatures are proved for every
     fragment of this set. As a consequence, the truth-table tell-tale has 13 + 1 data rather
     than 11 + 1, because ⊥ and ⊤ are constants of the language. The bilateral completeness
     theorems are proved for sequents over an arbitrary type of formulas.
   • Consequence operators are arbitrary closure operators. Finitarity is not assumed.
   • Learners, verifiers and certifiers are arbitrary, non-computable functions. Interactive
     lower bounds are proved against deterministic agents only.
   • Probability appears in two forms only. One is non-negative summable weights on a
     countable step set. The other is finite distributions, on the 2k worlds or on a finite class of
     labellers.
   • Real analysis. Smooth bumps are replaced by Lipschitz tents, because the compiled
     Mathlib subset does not include derivatives.

Not formalized.      The following are outside the formalization:
   • all probabilistic and anytime results, including Ville’s inequality and the Bayes-conservative
     verifiers (Section 4.4);
   • anti-unification and the schema-specific escalation bounds;
   • the noise results of Section 5;
   • arithmetic (Section 6.3);
   • the two-tier main theorem (Theorem 7.14);
   • the kink theorem and the other Kolmogorov-complexity results of Section 8;
   • first-order and real-parameter context semantics, with the exception of the export results
     listed below;
   • the Structured Physics Solution checker theorem;
   • the identification results of Section 10.
These rest on the paper proofs and on the adversarial verification described in Section 1.

Question                              Where and how it is answered
What is an appropriate learning       Least generalization over schemas, with a trimmed version
algorithm for inference rules         space for sporadic noise and cautious acceptance (Sections 4
(learning from positive examples)?    and 5). MDL is the wrong bias for positive data
                                      (Proposition 3.9).
Does that plus coherence get formal   Yes for classical propositional logic and complete decidable
math working?                         theories (Sections 6 and 7). Arithmetic is Popperian. There
                                      are sharp limits at Σ2 and for Turing progressions.
Would a setup have worked before      A bounded-gap verifier over latent formalizations is sound
we knew how to formalize proofs?      against adaptive provers and identifies validity exactly up to
                                      informal-validity equivalence. Counterexample objects are
                                      exponentially more informative than paradoxes, and
                                      Frege→Russell→Zermelo comes out as constrained repair
                                      (Section 10). The hardest open problem is affordable soundness
                                      when the learner must invent language.
How should contexts be handled,       Contexts are filters of models, and coherence is enforced only
and what about contradictory          on the root and anchored contexts (Theorem 11.12).
idealizations (“air pressure = 0”)?   Designating “idealization + full background” as coherent forces
                                      a globally paraconsistent logic (Proposition 11.10). Reductio
                                      needs a consistency certificate (Theorem 11.18).
“Check that a claim is true in the    Supertruth: true in every admissible completion of the
context at hand. But wtf is that?”    intended model, to the demanded precision (Theorem 11.39).
Can physics olympiad solutions be     A structured-solution checker that is sound against adversarial
checked in a principled way?          solvers, relative to an explicit judgment layer that is provably
                                      ineliminable (Theorems 11.37 and 11.38). There is a worked
                                      EuPhO example (Section 11.7).
Does a steeper simplicity penalty     Partly. The kink intuition is vindicated for product models.
recover intended rules rather than    But selection is a rate threshold with provable pathologies, and
teacher errors?                       the threshold is not identifiable from imitation data
                                      (Section 8).
Is there a “philosophical             Yes in the weak sense, and no in the intended sense (Section 9).
completeness theorem”?                Local coherence constraints of the kind used in
                                      consistency-based probing do not characterize coherence.
How come mathematicians ended up      Through local checkability on objects, plus completeness, plus
with such a nice notion of proof?     a bounded repertoire. Monster pressure leaves exactly the steps
                                      valid in every model of the theory, and Gödel completeness
                                      turns those into derivations (Proposition 10.38). Practice used
                                      only the robust core of its vague concepts (Theorem 10.35).
